`src/Type/CustomerPayment/CustomerPaymentType.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace FA\GraphQL\Type\CustomerPayment;

use Anorm\DataMapper;
use Anorm\GraphQL\Builder\FieldBuilder;
use Anorm\GraphQL\Mapper;
use DI\Container;
use FA\GraphQL\Fa\Service\CustomerPaymentService;
use FA\GraphQL\Fa\Service\FaIncludes;
use FA\GraphQL\Fa\Service\ServiceCall;
use FA\GraphQL\Model\AllocationModel;
use FA\GraphQL\Type\Allocation\AllocationType;
use FA\GraphQL\Type\CustomerPayment\Base\CustomerPaymentTypeBase;
use GraphQL\Type\Definition\Type;

/**
 * Yours to edit: anorm-graphql writes this file once and never again.
 *
 * Customer payments: debtor_trans of type 12 (scope()), written only through
 * FrontAccounting (CustomerPaymentService). The bank side is read from bank_trans
 * and gl_trans; allocations from cust_allocations (Release 3 spec section 5).
 * Delete voids.
 */
class CustomerPaymentType extends CustomerPaymentTypeBase
{
    private AllocationType $allocationType;

    public function __construct(AllocationType $allocationType)
    {
        $this->allocationType = $allocationType;
        parent::__construct();
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, string>
     */
    protected function areas(): array
    {
        return [
            self::VERB_LIST => 'SA_SALESTRANSVIEW',
            self::VERB_CREATE => 'SA_SALESPAYMNT',
            self::VERB_EDIT => 'SA_SALESALLOC',
            self::VERB_DELETE => 'SA_VOIDTRANSACTION',
        ];
    }

    protected function scope(): array
    {
        return ['transType' => CustomerPaymentService::TRANS_TYPE];
    }

    protected function fields(): array
    {
        return array_merge(parent::fields(), [
            FieldBuilder::create('allocations', Type::nonNull(Type::listOf(Type::nonNull($this->allocationType))))
                ->setDescription('What this payment is allocated to.')
                ->setResolver(function (array $row, $args, $context): array {
                    return $row['allocations'] ?? self::allocationsOf((int) $row['id'], $context);
                })
                ->build(),
            FieldBuilder::create('unallocated', Type::nonNull(Type::float()))
                ->setDescription('Amount plus discount, less what is allocated.')
                ->setResolver(function (array $row): float {
                    return round((float) $row['amount'] + (float) $row['discount'] - (float) $row['allocated'], 2);
                })
                ->build(),
            FieldBuilder::create('bankAccountId', Type::id())
                ->setResolver(function (array $row, $args, $context): ?string {
                    $bank = self::bankOf((int) $row['id'], $context);

                    return $bank === null ? null : (string) $bank['bank_act'];
                })
                ->build(),
            FieldBuilder::create('bankAmount', Type::float())
                ->setDescription('In the bank account\'s currency, before the charge.')
                ->setResolver(function (array $row, $args, $context): ?float {
                    $bank = self::bankOf((int) $row['id'], $context);

                    return $bank === null ? null : round((float) $bank['amount'] + self::chargeOf((int) $row['id']), 2);
                })
                ->build(),
            FieldBuilder::create('charge', Type::nonNull(Type::float()))
                ->setResolver(function (array $row): float {
                    return round(self::chargeOf((int) $row['id']), 2);
                })
                ->build(),
            FieldBuilder::create('memo', Type::string())
                ->setResolver(function (array $row, $args, $context): ?string {
                    $statement = $context->get(\PDO::class)->prepare(
                        'SELECT memo_ FROM ' . \FA\GraphQL\Fa\CompanyContext::prefix() . 'comments WHERE type = 12 AND id = ?'
                    );
                    $statement->execute([(int) $row['id']]);
                    $memo = $statement->fetchColumn();

                    return $memo === false ? null : (string) $memo;
                })
                ->build(),
            FieldBuilder::create('voided', Type::nonNull(Type::boolean()))
                ->setResolver(function (array $row, $args, $context): bool {
                    $statement = $context->get(\PDO::class)->prepare(
                        'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ' . \FA\GraphQL\Fa\CompanyContext::prefix() . 'voided WHERE type = 12 AND id = ?'
                    );
                    $statement->execute([(int) $row['id']]);

                    return (int) $statement->fetchColumn() > 0;
                })
                ->build(),
        ]);
    }

    public function resolveCreate($root, $args, Container $context): array
    {
        $this->authorize(self::VERB_CREATE, null, $context);
        $service = $context->get(CustomerPaymentService::class);
        $ids = ServiceCall::each($args['input'], function (array $input) use ($service): int {
            return $service->create($input);
        });

        return $this->rowsById($context, $ids, self::VERB_CREATE);
    }

    public function resolveUpdate($root, $args, Container $context): array
    {
        $this->authorize(self::VERB_EDIT, null, $context);
        $service = $context->get(CustomerPaymentService::class);
        $ids = ServiceCall::each($args['input'], function (array $input) use ($service): int {
            $input['id'] = self::intId($input['id'] ?? null);
            $service->update($input);

            return $input['id'];
        });

        return $this->rowsById($context, $ids, self::VERB_EDIT);
    }

    /**
     * Voids (spec section 5): returns each payment as it was, allocations included.
     */
    public function resolveDelete($root, $args, Container $context): array
    {
        $this->authorize(self::VERB_DELETE, null, $context);
        $ids = self::intIds($args['id']);
        $before = [];
        foreach ($this->rowsById($context, $ids, self::VERB_DELETE) as $index => $row) {
            $row['allocations'] = self::allocationsOf($ids[$index], $context);
            $before[] = $row;
        }
        $service = $context->get(CustomerPaymentService::class);
        ServiceCall::each($ids, function (int $id) use ($service): void {
            $service->delete($id);
        });

        return $before;
    }

    /**
     * Read directly, as SalesOrderType::linesOf(): the payment's own read already
     * passed its area.
     *
     * @param mixed $context the container
     * @return array<int, array<string, mixed>>
     */
    public static function allocationsOf(int $paymentNo, $context): array
    {
        $rows = [];
        $allocations = DataMapper::find(AllocationModel::class, $context->get(\PDO::class))
            ->where('trans_type_from = :type AND trans_no_from = :id', [':type' => CustomerPaymentService::TRANS_TYPE, ':id' => $paymentNo])
            ->orderBy('id')
            ->some();
        foreach ($allocations as $allocation) {
            $rows[] = Mapper::toArray($allocation);
        }

        return $rows;
    }

    /**
     * @param mixed $context
     * @return array{bank_act: string, amount: string}|null
     */
    private static function bankOf(int $paymentNo, $context): ?array
    {
        $statement = $context->get(\PDO::class)->prepare(
            'SELECT bank_act, amount FROM ' . \FA\GraphQL\Fa\CompanyContext::prefix()
            . 'bank_trans WHERE type = 12 AND trans_no = ? ORDER BY id LIMIT 1'
        );
        $statement->execute([$paymentNo]);
        $row = $statement->fetch(\PDO::FETCH_ASSOC);

        return $row === false ? null : $row;
    }

    /**
     * FrontAccounting does not store the charge; get_cust_bank_charge()
     * (payment_db.inc:142) recovers it from the GL.
     */
    private static function chargeOf(int $paymentNo): float
    {
        FaIncludes::billing();

        return (float) get_cust_bank_charge(ST_CUSTPAYMENT, $paymentNo);
    }
}
```

namespace FA\GraphQL\Type\Allocation;

    public function testLifecycle(): void
    {
        $created = $this->execute(
            'mutation ($i: [CustomerPaymentCreateInput!]!) { customerPaymentCreate(input: $i) { id amount unallocated voided } }',
            ['i' => [['customerId' => '1', 'branchId' => '1', 'bankAccountId' => '1', 'date' => date('Y-m-d'), 'amount' => 7.5]]]

        $this->assertSame(7.5, $created['unallocated']);
        $this->assertFalse($created['voided']);

        $listed = $this->execute(
            'query ($s: String) { customerPaymentList(query: {selector: $s}) { id } }',
            ['s' => json_encode(['id' => (int) $created['id']])]
        )['customerPaymentList'];
        $this->assertSame([$created['id']], array_column($listed, 'id'));

        $updated = $this->execute(
            'mutation ($i: [CustomerPaymentUpdateInput!]!) { customerPaymentUpdate(input: $i) { id allocations { id } } }',
            ['i' => [['id' => $created['id'], 'allocations' => []]]]
        )['customerPaymentUpdate'][0];
        $this->assertSame([], $updated['allocations']);

        $deleted = $this->execute(
            'mutation ($id: [ID!]!) { customerPaymentDelete(id: $id) { id } }',
            ['id' => [$created['id']]]
        )['customerPaymentDelete'];
        $this->assertSame([$created['id']], array_column($deleted, 'id'));
    }

### Checkpoint C — after Task 5 (payments and allocations)

Payments move money and allocations decide what a customer still owes. This checkpoint is placed here because a wrong allocation, a double-posted exchange variation or a payment that voids without freeing its invoices is expensive to find later, and Tasks 6–7 build the billing flow on top of it. It reviews everything since Checkpoint B (Task 5) independently — not by the implementer.

- [ ] Run `/code-review medium` (or an independent reviewer subagent with a review package of the diff since Checkpoint B's last commit) over the module's diff since Checkpoint B. Fix confirmed findings; commit as `Address Release 3 Checkpoint C review`, then a scoped re-review of the fix.
- [ ] **Spec compliance — §5, walked requirement by requirement**, each confirmed with the test (or evidence) that proves it, or the deviation written into the spec marked *(revised)*:
  - `customerPaymentCreate`: every input field of §5 (`customerId`, `branchId` optional only for a customer without branches, `bankAccountId`, `date`, `reference`, `amount > 0`, `discount`, `bankAmount` defaulting to `amount` only when the currencies match, `charge >= 0` and `!= amount` with a bank charge account, `memo`, `allocations`); each `can_process()` check of `sales/customer_payments.php:141-227` ported with its line; fiscal year and both exchange rates (spec §2.2, §2.3); the document lock held (§2.1).
  - Allocations: written through FrontAccounting's allocation cart with every item's `current_allocated` set explicitly — **never** the cart's remainder auto-assignment (`allocation_cart.inc:185-202`); `check_allocations()`'s rules ported (each `>= 0`, each `<=` outstanding, the invoice is the customer's, total `<= amount + discount + allocation_settled_allowance()`); only invoices are targets; a duplicate target refused.
  - `customerPaymentUpdate`: only `allocations` accepted, every other field `BAD_INPUT` naming it; the list replaces the allocations; `[]` deallocates; a foreign-currency payment that already has allocations refused (ruling 9) with no GL written.
  - `customerPaymentDelete`: FrontAccounting's `void_transaction()`, which clears the allocations and frees the invoices; FrontAccounting's bank-balance refusal surfaces as `FA_REJECTED`; unknown, voided, or non-payment numbers are `NOT_FOUND`; returns the payments as they were.
  - Reads: `CustomerPaymentType`'s `allocations`, `unallocated`, `bankAccountId`, `bankAmount`, `charge` (and `memo`, `voided` if kept — record them in §5 if the spec does not list them); `allocationList`; `CustomerType.balance` from `get_customer_details()`, and it needs `SA_SALESTRANSVIEW` beyond the customer's own area (record this rule in §5 if it is not there).
  - Areas: list `SA_SALESTRANSVIEW`, create `SA_SALESPAYMNT` (3080), edit `SA_SALESALLOC` (3081), delete `SA_VOIDTRANSACTION` (769); the seed role holds them; a write's read-back is authorised by the write's area (Release 2 ruling).

### Task 6: Emailing invoices: `bin/fa-report`, `invoiceEmail`, the stack's mail catcher

`rep107.php` includes `includes/session.inc` (both upstream and the fork), so it can never run in the API process. `bin/fa-report` runs it in a PHP CLI child process, exactly as FrontAccounting's print links do (`reporting/prn_redirect.php`). It logs in through the module's own `hook_authenticate`, so no password is involved. `InvoiceMailer` starts the child, waits at most 60 s, and turns the child's result line into `InvoiceEmailResult`. The stack catches mail as `.eml` files, and the tests read them.

**Files:**
- Create: `bin/fa-report` (executable, PHP, no extension), `src/Fa/Service/ReportRunner.php`, `src/Fa/Service/ReportRun.php`, `src/Fa/Service/InvoiceMailer.php`, `src/Type/Billing/InvoiceEmailResultType.php`, `docker/fa-mail-catcher`, `tests/Support/MailCatcher.php`
- Modify:
  - `src/ApiSchema.php`: hand-written `invoiceEmail`, no marker.
  - `.htaccess`: `bin/` denied explicitly.
  - `docker/Dockerfile`: the catcher script and `/var/mail-catcher`.
  - `docker/php.ini`: `sendmail_path`.
  - `docker/fa-graphql`: a `mail` command and help text.
  - `tests/data/seed.sql`: company email.
  - `phpcs.xml` and `phpstan.neon`: add `bin/fa-report`.
  - `tests/Unit/ApiSchemaTest.php`: the exact mutation set gains `invoiceEmail`.
  - `docker/README.md`.
- Test:
  - `tests/Unit/Fa/Service/InvoiceMailerTest.php`: result parsing and a fake runner.
  - `tests/Unit/Fa/Service/ReportRunnerTest.php`: real child processes, the timeout.
  - `tests/Integration/Billing/InvoiceEmailTest.php`: the real `rep107` child and the catcher.
  - `tests/Http/StackTest.php`: `bin/fa-report` is not served.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `InvoiceService::create(array $input): int` (Task 4, with `orderId`/`orderVersion`/`date`);
  - `SalesOrderService::create`, `CustomerService::create` (Release 2);
  - `FaBillingRows`, `FaOrderRows`, `FaTestRows` (cleanup);
  - `FaSession::user()`, `CompanyContext::company()`, `IntKey::parse($value, string $field)`;
  - `NotFound(string $message, ?int $index)`, `FaRejected(string $message, array $messages = [], ?int $index = null)`;
  - `Guard::require(string $area)`; `Config::fromFile(string $path)` and `$config->faRoot`; `Bootstrap::defaultRoot()`.
- Produces:
  - `bin/fa-report 107 <company> <login> <invoice-no> email`. It prints, as its last recognisable line, `FA_REPORT_RESULT {"messages":[{"level":"error|warning|notice","text":"..."}]}`. Exit code 0 means the report ran; anything else means refused usage (2) or a web SAPI (1). It refuses any SAPI but `cli`.
  - `ReportRun` has public `string $stdout`, `string $stderr`, `?int $exitCode`, `bool $timedOut`. `ReportRunner::run(array $argv, int $timeoutSeconds): ReportRun` takes an argument array (never a shell string), reads stdout and stderr together, and kills the child on timeout.
  - `InvoiceMailer::__construct(ReportRunner $runner, FaSession $session, ?string $script = null, int $timeoutSeconds = 60)`.
  - `InvoiceMailer::send(array $ids): array`: one result per id, in order, each `['id' => int, 'sent' => bool, 'recipient' => ?string, 'messages' => string[]]`.
  - `InvoiceMailer::interpret(ReportRun $run, int $timeoutSeconds): array` (public static, for tests): `['sent', 'recipient', 'messages']`.
  - `InvoiceMailer::RESULT_PREFIX = 'FA_REPORT_RESULT '`.
  - GraphQL: `invoiceEmail(id: [ID!]!): [InvoiceEmailResult!]!`, with `type InvoiceEmailResult { id: ID! sent: Boolean! recipient: String messages: [String!]! }`. Guarded by `SA_SALESTRANSVIEW` and `SA_SALESINVOICE`.
  - Stack: `/var/mail-catcher/*.eml`, and `docker/fa-graphql mail [list|show <file>|clear]`.

**Read before writing the child** (upstream `git -C /home/cambell/src/sgw/frontaccounting show upstream/master:<path>`; the fork differs only where noted):
- `reporting/prn_redirect.php:16-19` sets `$path_to_root = ".."`, `global $page_security; $page_security = 'SA_OPEN'`, and includes `../includes/session.inc`, all relative to the working directory.
- `prn_redirect.php:21-28`: when `user_save_report_selections() > 0` it calls `setcookie()`, which is harmless in CLI.
- `prn_redirect.php:44-58`: with `$_POST['REP_ID']` set it goes straight to `find_custom_file("/reporting/rep$rep.php")` and `require`s it, so a company's or extension's `rep107` override is used. It ends with `exit()`.
- `includes/session.inc` (upstream):
  - :401 `SessionManager->sessionStart()`, which is `session_start()`; a session file in CLI, no cookie.
  - :481-482 `register_shutdown_function('end_flush'); ob_start('output_html',0);`.
  - :499-501 includes `company/<user_company()>/installed_extensions.php` and calls `install_hooks()` before login.
  - :538-541 `current_user::login($_POST["company_login_name"], $_POST["user_name_entry_field"], $_POST["password"])`, then `ui_mode = $_POST['ui_mode']` (:543).
  - A failed login calls `login_fail()` (:547), which prints the login page and `exit`s.
- `includes/current_user.inc` `login()` calls `hook_authenticate($loginname, $password)` first, then `write_login_filelog()` when `login_delay > 0` (:76-82 upstream). The latter rewrites `tmp/faillog.php`, resetting the web UI's brute-force counter; this is what Release 1's Checkpoint B, finding I-2, forbade for token logins. **The child snapshots `tmp/faillog.php` before and restores it at shutdown.**
- `hooks.php` (module): `authenticate()` answers `true` only while `VerifiedIdentity::matches(user_company(), $login)`. `hooks.php` includes the same `vendor/autoload.php` the child requires, so the static flag is shared.
- `reporting/rep107.php:12-13`: `$page_security` is `SA_SALESTRANSVIEW` when `PARAM_0 == PARAM_1`. `print_invoices()` reads `PARAM_0..7`, and `PARAM_0`/`PARAM_1` are `"<no>-10"` with the suffix stripped. `PARAM_3 = 1` means email.
- `reporting/includes/pdf_report.inc` `End()`:
  - no contact with an email: `display_warning("You have no email contact defined …")`;
  - success: `display_notification("<title> <no> has been sent by email to destination. Email: <addresses>")`;
  - failure: `display_warning("… Sending document by email failed. Email: …")`.
  - These land in the global `$messages` as `[errno, text, file, line, bt]` (`includes/errors.inc` `error_handler`).
- `includes/errors.inc` `end_flush()` flushes every buffer at shutdown. The child's own shutdown function is registered **before** `session.inc` runs, so it runs first: it discards the buffers and writes the result line to `STDOUT` (`fwrite(STDOUT)` bypasses output buffering). `end_flush` then has nothing left to flush.
- **Multi-company caveat:** before login, `session.inc` installs the hooks of `user_company()`, the default company, not of the company being logged into. `hook_authenticate` therefore reaches `hooks_graphql` only if the graphql extension is active in the default company. FrontAccounting's own login has the same shape. Record it in the report; the stack's company is 0.

- [ ] **Step 1: The stack's mail catcher**

`docker/fa-mail-catcher` (new, `chmod 0755` in git: `git update-index --chmod=+x`):

```sh
#!/bin/sh
# sendmail_path for the GraphQL module's test stack. PHP's mail() pipes the whole
# message (To:, Subject:, headers, body) here instead of to a mail transport; it is
# kept as one .eml file, which the tests and `docker/fa-graphql mail` read. PHP
# passes its own -t -i style arguments on some builds; they are ignored.
set -eu
umask 000
dir=/var/mail-catcher
name="$(date +%Y%m%d%H%M%S)-$$-$(od -An -N4 -tx4 /dev/urandom | tr -d ' \n')"
cat > "$dir/.$name.tmp"
mv "$dir/.$name.tmp" "$dir/$name.eml"
```

`docker/Dockerfile`, after the two `COPY php.ini …` lines:

```dockerfile
# Mail from the stack never leaves it: PHP's sendmail_path (php.ini) is this
# script, which keeps every message as a file for the tests. Writable by Apache
# (www-data) and by the CLI user alike, and not sticky, so either can clear it.
COPY fa-mail-catcher /usr/local/bin/fa-mail-catcher
RUN set -eux; \
    chmod 0755 /usr/local/bin/fa-mail-catcher; \
    mkdir -p /var/mail-catcher; \
    chmod 0777 /var/mail-catcher
```

`docker/php.ini`, at the end:

```ini
; Every message PHP's mail() sends — FrontAccounting's invoice emails included —
; is kept as a file in /var/mail-catcher instead of being delivered. Development
; only; a live server keeps its own sendmail_path.
sendmail_path = /usr/local/bin/fa-mail-catcher
```

`docker/fa-graphql`: a command, beside `cmd_logs`:

```bash
# The stack's caught mail (docker/fa-mail-catcher): what FrontAccounting "sent".
cmd_mail() {
    ensure_running
    case "${1:-list}" in
        list)  c_exec_user sh -c 'ls -1t /var/mail-catcher/*.eml 2>/dev/null || echo "(no mail)"' ;;
        show)  [ $# -ge 2 ] || die "usage: fa-graphql mail show <file>"
               c_exec_user cat "/var/mail-catcher/$(basename "$2")" ;;
        clear) c_exec_user sh -c 'rm -f /var/mail-catcher/*.eml' ;;
        *)     die "usage: fa-graphql mail [list|show <file>|clear]" ;;
    esac
}
```

Add the dispatch line `    mail) cmd_mail "$@" ;;` and a help line under "Testing and development": `  mail [list|show <f>|clear]  Mail the stack caught instead of sending`.

WHERE `name` = 'email' AND (`value` IS NULL OR `value` = '');

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing tests**

`tests/Support/MailCatcher.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace FA\GraphQL\Tests\Support;

/**
 * The stack's caught mail (docker/fa-mail-catcher). A test snapshots the files
 * before it sends, reads only the new ones, and deletes only those.
 */
final class MailCatcher
{
    public const DIR = '/var/mail-catcher';

    /** @return string[] full paths of every caught message */
    public static function files(): array
    {
        $files = glob(self::DIR . '/*.eml') ?: [];
        sort($files);
        return $files;
    }

    /**
     * @param string[] $before
     * @return string[]
     */
    public static function newSince(array $before): array
    {
        return array_values(array_diff(self::files(), $before));
    }

    /** @param string[] $files */
    public static function delete(array $files): void
    {
        foreach ($files as $file) {
            @unlink($file);
        }
    }

    public static function available(): bool
    {
        return is_dir(self::DIR) && ini_get('sendmail_path') === '/usr/local/bin/fa-mail-catcher';
    }
}
```

`tests/Unit/Fa/Service/InvoiceMailerTest.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace FA\GraphQL\Tests\Unit\Fa\Service;

use FA\GraphQL\Fa\Service\InvoiceMailer;
use FA\GraphQL\Fa\Service\ReportRun;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

class InvoiceMailerTest extends TestCase
{
    private function run1(string $stdout, bool $timedOut = false, ?int $exit = 0): ReportRun
    {
        $run = new ReportRun();
        $run->stdout = $stdout;
        $run->stderr = '';
        $run->exitCode = $exit;
        $run->timedOut = $timedOut;
        return $run;
    }

    private function line(array $messages): string
    {
        return InvoiceMailer::RESULT_PREFIX . json_encode(['messages' => $messages]) . "\n";
    }

    public function testASentNotificationIsSentWithItsRecipient(): void
    {
        $r = InvoiceMailer::interpret($this->run1($this->line([
            ['level' => 'notice', 'text' => 'INVOICE 12 has been sent by email to destination. Email: a.b@example.com'],
        ])), 60);
        $this->assertTrue($r['sent']);
        $this->assertSame('a.b@example.com', $r['recipient']);
        $this->assertSame(['INVOICE 12 has been sent by email to destination. Email: a.b@example.com'], $r['messages']);
    }

    public function testAWarningMeansNotSent(): void
    {
        $r = InvoiceMailer::interpret($this->run1($this->line([
            ['level' => 'warning', 'text' => "You have no email contact defined for this type of document for 'Acme'."],
        ])), 60);
        $this->assertFalse($r['sent']);
        $this->assertNull($r['recipient']);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('no email contact', $r['messages'][0]);
    }

    public function testAnErrorBesideANotificationMeansNotSent(): void
    {
        $r = InvoiceMailer::interpret($this->run1($this->line([
            ['level' => 'notice', 'text' => 'sent. Email: x@example.com'],
            ['level' => 'error', 'text' => 'DATABASE ERROR'],
        ])), 60);
        $this->assertFalse($r['sent']);
    }

    public function testGarbageBeforeTheResultIsIgnored(): void
    {
        $out = "<html><body>Restricted</body></html>\nWarning: something\n"
            . $this->line([['level' => 'notice', 'text' => 'sent. Email: x@example.com']])
            . "trailing output from end_flush\n";
        $r = InvoiceMailer::interpret($this->run1($out), 60);
        $this->assertTrue($r['sent']);
        $this->assertSame('x@example.com', $r['recipient']);
    }

    public function testTheLastResultLineWins(): void
    {
        $out = $this->line([['level' => 'warning', 'text' => 'first']])
            . $this->line([['level' => 'notice', 'text' => 'sent. Email: y@example.com']]);
        $r = InvoiceMailer::interpret($this->run1($out), 60);
        $this->assertTrue($r['sent']);
        $this->assertSame('y@example.com', $r['recipient']);
    }

    public function testNoResultLineMeansNotSent(): void
    {
        $r = InvoiceMailer::interpret($this->run1("<html>login page</html>", false, 0), 60);
        $this->assertFalse($r['sent']);
        $this->assertSame(['The report process ended without a result.'], $r['messages']);
    }

    public function testATimedOutChildIsReported(): void
    {
        $r = InvoiceMailer::interpret($this->run1('', true, null), 60);
        $this->assertFalse($r['sent']);
        $this->assertSame(['The report process ended without a result.', 'It was stopped after 60 seconds.'], $r['messages']);
    }

    public function testAnUnreadableResultLineMeansNotSent(): void
    {
        $r = InvoiceMailer::interpret($this->run1(InvoiceMailer::RESULT_PREFIX . "{not json\n"), 60);
        $this->assertFalse($r['sent']);
        $this->assertSame(['The report process ended without a result.'], $r['messages']);
    }

    public function testTheArgvIsAnArrayWithTheCompanyLoginAndInvoice(): void
    {
        $this->assertSame(
            [PHP_BINARY, '/m/bin/fa-report', '107', '0', 'apitest', '12', 'email'],
            InvoiceMailer::argv('/m/bin/fa-report', 0, 'apitest', 12)
        );
    }
}
```

`tests/Unit/Fa/Service/ReportRunnerTest.php` (real processes, no FrontAccounting):

```php
<?php

namespace FA\GraphQL\Tests\Unit\Fa\Service;

use FA\GraphQL\Fa\Service\ReportRunner;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

class ReportRunnerTest extends TestCase
{
    public function testItCapturesStdoutStderrAndTheExitCode(): void
    {
        $run = (new ReportRunner())->run([PHP_BINARY, '-r', 'echo "out"; fwrite(STDERR, "err"); exit(3);'], 10);
        $this->assertSame('out', $run->stdout);
        $this->assertSame('err', $run->stderr);
        $this->assertSame(3, $run->exitCode);
        $this->assertFalse($run->timedOut);
    }

    public function testArgumentsAreNotInterpretedByAShell(): void
    {
        $run = (new ReportRunner())->run([PHP_BINARY, '-r', 'echo $argv[1];', '$(echo pwned); `id`'], 10);
        $this->assertSame('$(echo pwned); `id`', $run->stdout);
    }

    public function testATimedOutChildIsKilledAndReported(): void
    {
        $start = microtime(true);
        $run = (new ReportRunner())->run([PHP_BINARY, '-r', 'echo "started"; sleep(30);'], 1);
        $this->assertTrue($run->timedOut);
        $this->assertLessThan(10, microtime(true) - $start, 'the child must be stopped, not waited for');
        $this->assertSame('started', $run->stdout);
    }

    public function testLargeOutputOnBothStreamsDoesNotDeadlock(): void
    {
        $run = (new ReportRunner())->run(
            [PHP_BINARY, '-r', 'fwrite(STDERR, str_repeat("e", 200000)); echo str_repeat("o", 200000);'],
            10
        );
        $this->assertSame(200000, strlen($run->stdout));
        $this->assertSame(200000, strlen($run->stderr));
    }
}
```

`tests/Integration/Billing/InvoiceEmailTest.php` runs the real child against the stack. It extends `FaTestCase` in separate processes, with the setup pattern the billing tests of Tasks 3–5 use. **Read `tests/Integration/Billing/InvoiceServiceTest.php` (Task 4) first.** Reuse its fixture helper for "a customer with a branch, an order, and that order invoiced", if it has one, rather than writing another. The input keys below follow the generated names Releases 2–3 settled on (`locationId`, `taxNumber`, …); where Task 4's helper differs, use the helper.

```php
<?php

namespace FA\GraphQL\Tests\Integration\Billing;

use FA\GraphQL\Fa\Service\InvoiceMailer;
use FA\GraphQL\Tests\Integration\FaTestCase;
use FA\GraphQL\Tests\Support\MailCatcher;

/**
 * @runTestsInSeparateProcesses
 * @preserveGlobalState disabled
 */
class InvoiceEmailTest extends FaTestCase
{
    /** @var string[] */
    private array $mailBefore = [];

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        if (!MailCatcher::available()) {
            $this->markTestSkipped('The stack mail catcher is not installed (docker/fa-graphql up --build).');
        }
        $this->mailBefore = MailCatcher::files();
        // Session as apitest in company 0, and the billing cleanup snapshot, exactly as
        // InvoiceServiceTest::setUp does (Task 4).
        $this->enterAsApitest();
        $this->snapshotBillingRows();
    }

    protected function tearDown(): void
    {
        MailCatcher::delete(MailCatcher::newSince($this->mailBefore));
        $this->restoreBillingRows();   // FaBillingRows + FaOrderRows + FaTestRows, as Task 4
        parent::tearDown();
    }

    public function testAnInvoiceIsEmailedToTheCustomersContact(): void
    {
        $invoiceId = $this->invoiceForANewCustomer('gqlt-mail@example.com');
        $failLog = $this->faRoot() . '/tmp/faillog.php';
        $failLogBefore = is_file($failLog) ? file_get_contents($failLog) : null;

        $results = $this->container()->get(InvoiceMailer::class)->send([$invoiceId]);

        $this->assertCount(1, $results);
        $this->assertSame($invoiceId, $results[0]['id']);
        $this->assertTrue($results[0]['sent'], implode("\n", $results[0]['messages']));
        $this->assertSame('gqlt-mail@example.com', $results[0]['recipient']);

        $new = MailCatcher::newSince($this->mailBefore);
        $this->assertCount(1, $new);
        $eml = file_get_contents($new[0]);
        $this->assertMatchesRegularExpression('/^To: .*gqlt-mail@example\.com/m', $eml);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('Content-Disposition: attachment', $eml);
        $this->assertMatchesRegularExpression('/filename="[^"]*\.pdf"/', $eml);

        $this->assertSame(
            $failLogBefore,
            is_file($failLog) ? file_get_contents($failLog) : null,
            'the child logs in through hook_authenticate and must not reset the web UI throttle'
        );
    }

    public function testACustomerWithoutAnEmailContactIsNotSent(): void
    {
        $invoiceId = $this->invoiceForANewCustomer(null);

        $results = $this->container()->get(InvoiceMailer::class)->send([$invoiceId]);

        $this->assertFalse($results[0]['sent']);
        $this->assertNull($results[0]['recipient']);
        $this->assertStringContainsString('no email contact', implode("\n", $results[0]['messages']));
        $this->assertCount(0, MailCatcher::newSince($this->mailBefore));
    }

    public function testAnUnknownInvoiceIsNotFound(): void
    {
        $this->expectException(\FA\GraphQL\Error\NotFound::class);
        $this->container()->get(InvoiceMailer::class)->send([999999]);
    }

    public function testAVoidedInvoiceIsRefused(): void
    {
        $invoiceId = $this->invoiceForANewCustomer('gqlt-void@example.com');
        $this->voidInvoice($invoiceId);   // InvoiceService::delete, as Task 4's tests do

        $this->expectException(\FA\GraphQL\Error\FaRejected::class);
        $this->container()->get(InvoiceMailer::class)->send([$invoiceId]);
    }

    public function testItRefusesToRunInsideATransaction(): void
    {
        $invoiceId = $this->invoiceForANewCustomer('gqlt-tx@example.com');
        begin_transaction();
        try {
            $this->expectException(\LogicException::class);
            $this->container()->get(InvoiceMailer::class)->send([$invoiceId]);
        } finally {
            cancel_transaction();
        }
    }
}
```

`invoiceForANewCustomer(?string $email): int` is this test's fixture:
- `CustomerService::create`: a `gqlt-` reference, `branch` defaults from demo lookups, and `contact: ['email' => $email]` when given;
- then `SalesOrderService::create` with one stock line dated today;
- then `InvoiceService::create(['orderId' => …, 'orderVersion' => …, 'date' => today])`, returning the invoice number.

The Release 2 customer create links the contact to the customer and branch as `general`. `get_branch_contacts($branch, 'invoice', …)` falls back to `general`, so that contact receives the invoice. `enterAsApitest()`, `snapshotBillingRows()`, `restoreBillingRows()`, `container()`, `faRoot()` and `voidInvoice()` are Task 4's helpers; use their real names.

`src/Fa/Service/ReportRun.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace FA\GraphQL\Fa\Service;

/** What a child process left behind: its output, its exit code, and whether it was stopped. */
final class ReportRun
{
    public string $stdout = '';
    public string $stderr = '';
    public ?int $exitCode = null;
    public bool $timedOut = false;
}
```

`src/Fa/Service/ReportRunner.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace FA\GraphQL\Fa\Service;

/**
 * Runs a child process from an argument array — never a shell string, so nothing in
 * an argument is interpreted — reading stdout and stderr together (a child filling
 * one pipe while the parent waits on the other would deadlock), and stopping it at
 * the deadline.
 */
class ReportRunner
{
    /** @param string[] $argv */
    public function run(array $argv, int $timeoutSeconds): ReportRun
    {
        $run = new ReportRun();
        $pipes = [];
        $process = proc_open($argv, [0 => ['pipe', 'r'], 1 => ['pipe', 'w'], 2 => ['pipe', 'w']], $pipes);
        if (!is_resource($process)) {
            $run->stderr = 'The report process could not be started.';
            return $run;
        }
        fclose($pipes[0]);
        stream_set_blocking($pipes[1], false);
        stream_set_blocking($pipes[2], false);

        $deadline = microtime(true) + $timeoutSeconds;
        $open = [1 => $pipes[1], 2 => $pipes[2]];
        while ($open) {
            $left = $deadline - microtime(true);
            if ($left <= 0) {
                $run->timedOut = true;
                break;
            }
            $read = array_values($open);
            $write = null;
            $except = null;
            if (stream_select($read, $write, $except, (int) $left, (int) (fmod($left, 1) * 1000000)) === false) {
                break;
            }
            foreach ($open as $i => $pipe) {
                $chunk = fread($pipe, 65536);
                if ($chunk !== false && $chunk !== '') {
                    if ($i === 1) {
                        $run->stdout .= $chunk;
                    } else {
                        $run->stderr .= $chunk;
                    }
                }
                if (feof($pipe)) {
                    fclose($pipe);
                    unset($open[$i]);
                }
            }
        }
        foreach ($open as $pipe) {
            fclose($pipe);
        }
        if ($run->timedOut) {
            proc_terminate($process, 9);
        }
        $status = proc_close($process);
        $run->exitCode = $run->timedOut ? null : $status;
        return $run;
    }
}
```

`src/Fa/Service/InvoiceMailer.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace FA\GraphQL\Fa\Service;

use FA\GraphQL\Error\FaRejected;
use FA\GraphQL\Error\NotFound;
use FA\GraphQL\Fa\CompanyContext;
use FA\GraphQL\Fa\FaSession;

/**
 * Emails invoices through FrontAccounting's own invoice report (rep107), which can
 * only run in a process of its own: it includes session.inc (Release 3 spec §6).
 * One child per invoice, after the caller's writes have committed, stopped after
 * the timeout. The child's result line is the only thing read from its output.
 */
class InvoiceMailer
{
    public const RESULT_PREFIX = 'FA_REPORT_RESULT ';
    private const NO_RESULT = 'The report process ended without a result.';

    private ReportRunner $runner;
    private FaSession $session;
    private string $script;
    private int $timeoutSeconds;

    public function __construct(ReportRunner $runner, FaSession $session, ?string $script = null, int $timeoutSeconds = 60)
    {
        $this->runner = $runner;
        $this->session = $session;
        $this->script = $script ?? dirname(__DIR__, 3) . '/bin/fa-report';
        $this->timeoutSeconds = $timeoutSeconds;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<int, mixed> $ids
     * @return array<int, array{id: int, sent: bool, recipient: ?string, messages: string[]}>
     */
    public function send(array $ids): array
    {
        if (($GLOBALS['transaction_level'] ?? 0) > 0) {
            throw new \LogicException('Invoices are emailed after the transaction that wrote them has committed.');
        }
        FaIncludes::billing();
        $numbers = [];
        foreach (array_values($ids) as $index => $id) {
            $no = IntKey::parse($id, 'id');
            $this->assertEmailable($no, $index);
            $numbers[] = $no;
        }
        $login = (string) $this->session->user()->loginname;
        $company = CompanyContext::company();
        $results = [];
        foreach ($numbers as $no) {
            $run = $this->runner->run(self::argv($this->script, $company, $login, $no), $this->timeoutSeconds);
            $result = self::interpret($run, $this->timeoutSeconds);
            if (!$result['sent']) {
                error_log(sprintf(
                    'graphql: invoice %d not emailed; child exit %s; stdout %s; stderr %s',
                    $no,
                    $run->timedOut ? 'timeout' : (string) $run->exitCode,
                    substr($run->stdout, -2048),
                    substr($run->stderr, -2048)
                ));
            }
            $results[] = ['id' => $no] + $result;
        }
        return $results;
    }

    /** @return string[] */
    public static function argv(string $script, int $company, string $login, int $invoiceNo): array
    {
        return [PHP_BINARY, $script, '107', (string) $company, $login, (string) $invoiceNo, 'email'];
    }

    /** @return array{sent: bool, recipient: ?string, messages: string[]} */
    public static function interpret(ReportRun $run, int $timeoutSeconds): array
    {
        $payload = null;
        foreach (preg_split('/\r\n|\n|\r/', $run->stdout) ?: [] as $line) {
            if (strpos($line, self::RESULT_PREFIX) === 0) {
                $decoded = json_decode(substr($line, strlen(self::RESULT_PREFIX)), true);
                $payload = is_array($decoded) && isset($decoded['messages']) && is_array($decoded['messages'])
                    ? $decoded
                    : null;
            }
        }
        if ($payload === null) {
            $messages = [self::NO_RESULT];
            if ($run->timedOut) {
                $messages[] = "It was stopped after $timeoutSeconds seconds.";
            }
            return ['sent' => false, 'recipient' => null, 'messages' => $messages];
        }
        $texts = [];
        $notified = false;
        $failed = false;
        $recipient = null;
        foreach ($payload['messages'] as $message) {
            $text = trim((string) ($message['text'] ?? ''));
            $level = (string) ($message['level'] ?? 'error');
            $texts[] = $text;
            if ($level === 'notice') {
                $notified = true;
                if (preg_match_all('/[A-Z0-9._%+\-]+@[A-Z0-9.\-]+\.[A-Z]{2,}/i', $text, $m) && $m[0]) {
                    $recipient = end($m[0]);
                }
            } else {
                $failed = true;
            }
        }
        $sent = $notified && !$failed && !$run->timedOut;
        return ['sent' => $sent, 'recipient' => $sent ? $recipient : null, 'messages' => $texts];
    }

    private function assertEmailable(int $no, int $index): void
    {
        $sql = 'SELECT t.trans_no, v.id AS voided FROM ' . TB_PREF . 'debtor_trans t'
            . ' LEFT JOIN ' . TB_PREF . 'voided v ON v.type = t.type AND v.id = t.trans_no'
            . ' WHERE t.type = ' . ST_SALESINVOICE . ' AND t.trans_no = ' . db_escape($no);
        $row = db_fetch(db_query($sql, 'could not read the invoice'));
        if (!$row) {
            throw new NotFound("Invoice $no does not exist.", $index);
        }
        if ($row['voided'] !== null) {
            throw new FaRejected("Invoice $no is voided and cannot be emailed.", ["Invoice $no is voided."], $index);
        }
    }
}
```

`sent` is decided by level, not by wording, because FrontAccounting's texts are translated by the user's language. The recipient is whatever address the notification names.

Check two things against the code before relying on them:
- **`FaIncludes::billing()`:** it exists from Task 2, and it loads what `ST_SALESINVOICE` and the `voided` join need. `ST_SALESINVOICE` comes from `includes/types.inc`, which is already loaded.
- **The current user's login name:** `FaSession::user()->loginname` is the property that holds it. `current_user` has `loginname` in 2.4; if the fork or upstream differs, use what `ViewerType` uses.

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement the child**

`bin/fa-report` (make it executable: `chmod +x bin/fa-report && git update-index --chmod=+x bin/fa-report`):

```php
#!/usr/bin/env php
<?php

/*
 * Runs one FrontAccounting report the way its own print links do
 * (reporting/prn_redirect.php), for InvoiceMailer. It exists because rep107.php
 * includes session.inc, which the API process can never include (Release 3 spec §6).
 *
 * Usage: bin/fa-report 107 <company> <login> <invoice-no> email
 *
 * It logs in as <login> through the module's hooks_graphql::authenticate — the
 * same VerifiedIdentity the API uses for a verified token — so no password is
 * involved. The caller (InvoiceMailer) is the API, which has already authenticated
 * and authorised that user. Anyone able to run this script can already run PHP as
 * the web server's user; it takes no secret and prints none.
 *
 * Its last line of output starting with FA_REPORT_RESULT is the result: the
 * messages FrontAccounting raised, by level. Everything else it prints —
 * FrontAccounting's HTML on an early exit, warnings — is discarded or ignored.
 */

if (PHP_SAPI !== 'cli') {
    http_response_code(404);
    exit(1);
}

$faReportArgs = array_slice($argv, 1);
if (
    count($faReportArgs) !== 5
    || $faReportArgs[0] !== '107'
    || !ctype_digit($faReportArgs[1])
    || $faReportArgs[2] === ''
    || !ctype_digit($faReportArgs[3])
    || $faReportArgs[4] !== 'email'
) {
    fwrite(STDERR, "usage: fa-report 107 <company> <login> <invoice-no> email\n");
    exit(2);
}
$faReportCompany = (int) $faReportArgs[1];
$faReportLogin = $faReportArgs[2];
$faReportTransNo = (int) $faReportArgs[3];

$faReportModule = dirname(__DIR__);
require $faReportModule . '/vendor/autoload.php';

$faReportConfig = \FA\GraphQL\Config::fromFile($faReportModule . '/config_graphql.php');
$faReportRoot = $faReportConfig->faRoot !== '' ? $faReportConfig->faRoot : \FA\GraphQL\Fa\Bootstrap::defaultRoot();
if ($faReportRoot[0] !== '/') {
    $faReportRoot = $faReportModule . '/' . $faReportRoot;
}
$faReportRoot = realpath($faReportRoot) ?: $faReportRoot;

// The web UI's brute-force counter (Release 1, Checkpoint B I-2): current_user::login()
// rewrites it on every successful login. Put back exactly as it was.
$faReportFailLog = $faReportRoot . '/tmp/faillog.php';
$faReportFailLogBefore = is_file($faReportFailLog) ? (string) file_get_contents($faReportFailLog) : null;

// Registered before session.inc registers end_flush, so it runs first: it collects
// FrontAccounting's messages, discards whatever FrontAccounting buffered (HTML
// included), restores the fail log, and writes the one result line.
register_shutdown_function(function () use ($faReportFailLog, $faReportFailLogBefore) {
    $levels = [E_USER_ERROR => 'error', E_USER_WARNING => 'warning', E_USER_NOTICE => 'notice'];
    $collected = [];
    foreach ((array) ($GLOBALS['messages'] ?? []) as $message) {
        $level = $levels[$message[0] ?? 0] ?? 'error';
        $text = trim(html_entity_decode(strip_tags((string) ($message[1] ?? '')), ENT_QUOTES));
        if ($text !== '') {
            $collected[] = ['level' => $level, 'text' => $text];
        }
    }
    $fatal = error_get_last();
    if ($fatal !== null && in_array($fatal['type'], [E_ERROR, E_PARSE, E_CORE_ERROR, E_COMPILE_ERROR], true)) {
        $collected[] = ['level' => 'error', 'text' => 'PHP fatal error in the report: ' . $fatal['message']];
    }
    while (ob_get_level() > 0) {
        ob_end_clean();
    }
    if ($faReportFailLogBefore === null) {
        if (is_file($faReportFailLog)) {
            @unlink($faReportFailLog);
        }
    } elseif (!is_file($faReportFailLog) || file_get_contents($faReportFailLog) !== $faReportFailLogBefore) {
        @file_put_contents($faReportFailLog, $faReportFailLogBefore);
    }
    \FA\GraphQL\Fa\VerifiedIdentity::clear();
    if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_ACTIVE) {
        @session_destroy();
    }
    fwrite(STDOUT, 'FA_REPORT_RESULT ' . json_encode(['messages' => $collected]) . "\n");
});

\FA\GraphQL\Fa\VerifiedIdentity::set($faReportCompany, $faReportLogin);

$_SERVER['REMOTE_ADDR'] = $_SERVER['REMOTE_ADDR'] ?? '127.0.0.1';
$_SERVER['REQUEST_URI'] = $_SERVER['REQUEST_URI'] ?? '/reporting/prn_redirect.php';
$_GET = [];
$_COOKIE = [];
$_POST = [
    'user_name_entry_field' => $faReportLogin,
    'password' => '',
    'company_login_name' => (string) $faReportCompany,
    'ui_mode' => '1',
    'REP_ID' => '107',
    'PARAM_0' => $faReportTransNo . '-' . 10,
    'PARAM_1' => $faReportTransNo . '-' . 10,
    'PARAM_2' => '',
    'PARAM_3' => '1',
    'PARAM_4' => '',
    'PARAM_5' => '',
    'PARAM_6' => '',
    'PARAM_7' => '0',
];
$_REQUEST = $_POST;

// prn_redirect.php and rep107.php use paths relative to reporting/ (upstream's
// $path_to_root = ".."), and FrontAccounting's variables must be globals: included
// here, at file scope, not from a function.
chdir($faReportRoot . '/reporting');
ob_start();
include $faReportRoot . '/reporting/prn_redirect.php';
```

`.htaccess`: extend the directory rule to deny `bin/`, even though the "only `index.php`" rule already covers real files there:

```apache
RewriteRule ^(bin|sql|src|tests|vendor|docs|docker|tmp)/ - [F,L]
```

`phpcs.xml`: add `<file>bin/fa-report</file>`, and, if phpcs skips extensionless files, `<arg name="extensions" value="php,inc"/>` plus an explicit `--extensions` override for this file. Whichever way, `docker/fa-graphql lint` must check it. PSR-12 wants side-effect-free files; add `<rule ref="PSR1.Files.SideEffects"><exclude-pattern>bin/fa-report</exclude-pattern></rule>` if phpcs flags it. `phpstan.neon`: add `bin/fa-report` to `paths`.

- [ ] **Step 6: The GraphQL mutation**

`src/Type/Billing/InvoiceEmailResultType.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace FA\GraphQL\Type\Billing;

use GraphQL\Type\Definition\ObjectType;
use GraphQL\Type\Definition\Type;

/** One invoice's outcome from invoiceEmail. */
class InvoiceEmailResultType extends ObjectType
{
    public function __construct()
    {
        parent::__construct([
            'name' => 'InvoiceEmailResult',
            'fields' => [
                'id' => ['type' => Type::nonNull(Type::id())],
                'sent' => [
                    'type' => Type::nonNull(Type::boolean()),
                    'description' => 'FrontAccounting reported the invoice sent (handed to the mail transport).',
                ],
                'recipient' => [
                    'type' => Type::string(),
                    'description' => 'The address FrontAccounting sent it to, when sent.',
                ],
                'messages' => [
                    'type' => Type::nonNull(Type::listOf(Type::nonNull(Type::string()))),
                    'description' => "FrontAccounting's messages, e.g. why it was not sent.",
                ],
            ],
        ]);
    }
}
```

`src/ApiSchema.php`: in the mutation `fields` array, a hand-written entry without the `// anorm-graphql` marker, placed alphabetically among the existing entries (between `invoiceDelete`, if present, and `login`):

```php
                    'invoiceEmail' => [
                        'type' => Type::nonNull(Type::listOf(Type::nonNull($this->type(InvoiceEmailResultType::class)))),
                        'description' => "Email invoices through FrontAccounting's invoice report, to each "
                            . "customer's invoice (else general) contact. Runs after any writes in the same "
                            . 'request have committed.',
                        'args' => [
                            'id' => ['type' => Type::nonNull(Type::listOf(Type::nonNull(Type::id())))],
                        ],
                        'resolve' => function ($root, array $args) {
                            Guard::require('SA_SALESTRANSVIEW');
                            Guard::require('SA_SALESINVOICE');
                            return $this->type(InvoiceMailer::class)->send($args['id']);
                        },
                    ],
```

with the `use` lines `FA\GraphQL\Auth\Guard`, `FA\GraphQL\Fa\Service\InvoiceMailer` and `FA\GraphQL\Type\Billing\InvoiceEmailResultType`. GraphQL executes mutation fields in order, and each earlier field's `ServiceCall` has committed before this resolver runs, so `invoiceCreate` followed by `invoiceEmail` in one document works. `InvoiceMailer::send` refuses to run inside a transaction.

Run `bin/generate --dry-run` on the host. It must report `current` for `ApiSchema.php`, which proves the generator leaves the hand-written entry alone.

### Task 7: The billing flow over HTTP, and the README

The panel's billing, end to end through Apache and the real schema (spec §8, "HTTP"):
1. a customer with an email contact, and an order;
2. `invoiceCreate(orderId)`, which delivers and invoices in one step;
3. `invoiceEmail`;
4. `customerPaymentCreate`, allocated to the invoice, after which the invoice's `outstanding` and the customer's balance are 0;
5. `customerPaymentUpdate`, which deallocates it;
6. `customerPaymentDelete`, which voids the payment;
7. `invoiceDelete`, which voids the invoice.

A second test covers the partial path: `deliveryCreate` for part of an order, `invoiceCreate(deliveryIds)`, and the guards on voiding. A third covers permissions. The README documents the billing API.

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/Http/BillingFlowTest.php`
- Modify: `README.md` (sections "Deliveries and invoices", "Payments and allocations", "Emailing invoices", "The billing flow"), `docker/README.md` (the mail catcher, if Task 6 hasn't already documented it)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `GraphQLClient` (`gql`, `login`);
  - `FaOrderRows` (Release 2), `FaBillingRows` (Task 2), `FaTestRows` and `MailCatcher` (Task 6);
  - the generated root fields of Tasks 3–6: `deliveryCreate`, `deliveryDelete`, `deliveryList`, `invoiceCreate`, `invoiceDelete`, `invoiceList`, `invoiceEmail`, `customerPaymentCreate`, `customerPaymentUpdate`, `customerPaymentDelete`, `customerPaymentList`, `allocationList`, `bankAccountList`, and `customerList` with `balance`.
- Produces: nothing later tasks consume.

- [ ] **Step 1: Align with the schema and the demo data before writing the test**

The field names below follow the contract, but generation wins (Global Constraints). Introspect the running stack:

```bash
TOKEN=$(docker/fa-graphql exec php -r '
  $c = stream_context_create(["http" => ["method" => "POST", "header" => "Content-Type: application/json",
    "content" => json_encode(["query" => "mutation { login(user: \"apitest\", password: \"password\") { accessToken } }"])]]);
  echo json_decode(file_get_contents("http://localhost:8000/modules/graphql/", false, $c), true)["data"]["login"]["accessToken"];')
for t in InvoiceType CustomerPaymentType DeliveryType CustomerType InvoiceCreateInput DeliveryCreateInput CustomerPaymentCreateInput CustomerPaymentUpdateInput InvoiceEmailResult AllocationType; do
  docker/fa-graphql exec php -r '
    $c = stream_context_create(["http" => ["method" => "POST", "header" => "Content-Type: application/json\r\nAuthorization: Bearer '"$TOKEN"'",
      "content" => json_encode(["query" => "{ __type(name: \"'"$t"'\") { name fields { name } inputFields { name } } }"])]]);
    echo file_get_contents("http://localhost:8000/modules/graphql/", false, $c), "\n";'
done
```

Replace every field name in the test's constants that differs, including the shape of `balance` on `CustomerType` (a scalar or an object with its buckets), and record each difference in the report.

Pick two demo items:
- **A line item:**

  ```sql
  SELECT stock_id, mb_flag FROM 0_stock_master WHERE inactive = 0 AND no_sale = 0 AND mb_flag <> 'F' ORDER BY mb_flag = 'D' DESC, stock_id LIMIT 5
  ```

  Prefer a service item (`mb_flag = 'D'`). It has no stock check, so a delivery can't be refused for negative stock (`check_qoh()`), whatever the demo quantities. Use `docker/fa-graphql db shell`.
- **A bank account in the home currency:**

  ```sql
  SELECT id, bank_account_name, bank_curr_code FROM 0_bank_accounts WHERE inactive = 0
  ```

  compared against `SELECT value FROM 0_sys_prefs WHERE name = 'curr_default'`.

Put both in the test's constants `ITEM` and `BANK_ACCOUNT` with a comment naming the query.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the tests**

`tests/Http/BillingFlowTest.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace FA\GraphQL\Tests\Http;

use FA\GraphQL\Tests\Support\FaBillingRows;
use FA\GraphQL\Tests\Support\FaOrderRows;
use FA\GraphQL\Tests\Support\FaTestRows;
use FA\GraphQL\Tests\Support\MailCatcher;
use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;

/**
 * The panel's billing flow end to end (Release 3 spec §8): invoice an order in one
 * step, email it, take a payment allocated to it, deallocate, void the payment,
 * void the invoice; the partial-delivery path; and who may do it.
 *
 * tearDown removes everything a test wrote, pass or fail — customers by the test's
 * reference prefix (FaTestRows), orders (FaOrderRows), every billing row written
 * since setUp (FaBillingRows: debtor_trans, details, gl_trans, stock_moves,
 * bank_trans, cust_allocations, trans_tax_details, comments, refs, audit_trail,
 * voided), the refresh tokens its logins created, and the mail it caused — and
 * nothing an earlier run left.
 */
class BillingFlowTest extends TestCase
{
    use GraphQLClient;

    /** A sellable service item: no stock check on delivery (Step 1's query). */
    private const ITEM = 'REPLACE-IN-STEP-1';

    /** A home-currency bank account (Step 1's query). */
    private const BANK_ACCOUNT = 'REPLACE-IN-STEP-1';

    private const CUSTOMER_CREATE = 'mutation ($in: [CustomerCreateInput!]!) '
        . '{ customerCreate(input: $in) { id branches { id } } }';

    private const ORDER_CREATE = 'mutation ($in: [SalesOrderCreateInput!]!) '
        . '{ salesOrderCreate(input: $in) { id version } }';

    private const INVOICE_FIELDS = '{ id reference total outstanding voided deliveryIds '
        . 'lines { id stockId quantity unitPrice } }';

    private const INVOICE_CREATE = 'mutation ($in: [InvoiceCreateInput!]!) { invoiceCreate(input: $in) '
        . self::INVOICE_FIELDS . ' }';

    private const INVOICE_BY_ID = 'query ($q: MangoInput) { invoiceList(query: $q) ' . self::INVOICE_FIELDS . ' }';

    private const INVOICE_DELETE = 'mutation ($ids: [ID!]!) { invoiceDelete(id: $ids) { id } }';

    private const INVOICE_EMAIL = 'mutation ($ids: [ID!]!) { invoiceEmail(id: $ids) { id sent recipient messages } }';

    private const DELIVERY_CREATE = 'mutation ($in: [DeliveryCreateInput!]!) '
        . '{ deliveryCreate(input: $in) { id lines { id quantity qtyInvoiced } } }';

    private const DELIVERY_DELETE = 'mutation ($ids: [ID!]!) { deliveryDelete(id: $ids) { id } }';

    private const PAYMENT_FIELDS = '{ id amount unallocated allocations { toId amount } }';

    private const PAYMENT_CREATE = 'mutation ($in: [CustomerPaymentCreateInput!]!) '
        . '{ customerPaymentCreate(input: $in) ' . self::PAYMENT_FIELDS . ' }';

    private const PAYMENT_UPDATE = 'mutation ($in: [CustomerPaymentUpdateInput!]!) '
        . '{ customerPaymentUpdate(input: $in) ' . self::PAYMENT_FIELDS . ' }';

    private const PAYMENT_DELETE = 'mutation ($ids: [ID!]!) { customerPaymentDelete(id: $ids) { id } }';

    private const CUSTOMER_BALANCE = 'query ($q: MangoInput) { customerList(query: $q) { id balance } }';

    private string $token;
    private string $prefix;
    /** @var int[] */
    private array $orders = [];
    private int $tokenMark = 0;
    /** @var string[] */
    private array $mailBefore = [];
    private ?FaOrderRows $orderRows = null;
    private ?FaBillingRows $billingRows = null;
    private ?\PDO $pdo = null;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        $this->prefix = 'BILL-' . bin2hex(random_bytes(4));
        $this->orderRows = FaOrderRows::mark($this->pdo(), $this->table(''));
        $this->billingRows = FaBillingRows::mark($this->pdo(), $this->table(''));
        $this->tokenMark = (int) $this->pdo()->query(
            'SELECT COALESCE(MAX(id), 0) FROM ' . $this->table('graphql_refresh_token')
        )->fetchColumn();
        $this->mailBefore = MailCatcher::available() ? MailCatcher::files() : [];
        $this->token = $this->login()['accessToken'];
    }

    protected function tearDown(): void
    {
        if ($this->orderRows === null) {
            return;
        }
        // Billing rows first: they reference the orders and customers.
        $this->billingRows->purge();
        foreach (array_unique(array_merge($this->orders, $this->ordersOfTestCustomers())) as $orderNo) {
            $this->orderRows->purge($orderNo);
        }
        FaTestRows::sweep($this->pdo(), $this->prefix, $this->table(''));
        $this->pdo()->prepare('DELETE FROM ' . $this->table('graphql_refresh_token') . ' WHERE id > ?')
            ->execute([$this->tokenMark]);
        if (MailCatcher::available()) {
            MailCatcher::delete(MailCatcher::newSince($this->mailBefore));
        }
        $this->orders = [];
        $this->orderRows = null;
        $this->billingRows = null;
    }

    public function testTheBillingFlow(): void
    {
        $customer = $this->createCustomer();
        $order = $this->createOrder($customer, 2, 40.0);

        // Invoice the order in one step: FrontAccounting delivers what remains, then invoices it.
        $invoice = $this->ok(self::INVOICE_CREATE, ['in' => [[
            'orderId' => $order['id'],
            'orderVersion' => $order['version'],
            'date' => $this->today(),
        ]]])['invoiceCreate'][0];
        $this->assertGreaterThan(0, (float) $invoice['total']);
        $this->assertEqualsWithDelta((float) $invoice['total'], (float) $invoice['outstanding'], 0.001);
        $this->assertCount(1, $invoice['deliveryIds'], 'one-step invoicing makes one delivery');
        $this->assertCount(1, $invoice['lines']);
        $this->assertFalse($invoice['voided']);
        $total = (float) $invoice['total'];
        $this->assertEqualsWithDelta($total, $this->balance($customer['id']), 0.001);

        // Email it: the customer's contact has an email address.
        if (MailCatcher::available()) {
            $sent = $this->ok(self::INVOICE_EMAIL, ['ids' => [$invoice['id']]])['invoiceEmail'][0];
            $this->assertTrue($sent['sent'], implode("\n", $sent['messages']));
            $this->assertStringEndsWith('@example.com', (string) $sent['recipient']);
            $this->assertCount(1, MailCatcher::newSince($this->mailBefore));
        }

        // Pay it in full, allocated to the invoice.
        $payment = $this->ok(self::PAYMENT_CREATE, ['in' => [[
            'customerId' => $customer['id'],
            'branchId' => $customer['branches'][0]['id'],
            'bankAccountId' => self::BANK_ACCOUNT,
            'date' => $this->today(),
            'amount' => $total,
            'allocations' => [['invoiceId' => $invoice['id'], 'amount' => $total]],
        ]]])['customerPaymentCreate'][0];
        $this->assertEqualsWithDelta(0.0, (float) $payment['unallocated'], 0.001);
        $this->assertCount(1, $payment['allocations']);
        $this->assertEqualsWithDelta(0.0, (float) $this->readInvoice($invoice['id'])['outstanding'], 0.001);
        $this->assertEqualsWithDelta(0.0, $this->balance($customer['id']), 0.001);

        // An allocated invoice cannot be voided (spec §4, ruling 3).
        $refused = $this->gql(self::INVOICE_DELETE, ['ids' => [$invoice['id']]], $this->token);
        $this->assertSame('FA_REJECTED', $this->code($refused), $refused['raw']);

        // Deallocate: the payment is unallocated, the invoice outstanding again.
        $payment = $this->ok(self::PAYMENT_UPDATE, ['in' => [['id' => $payment['id'], 'allocations' => []]]])
            ['customerPaymentUpdate'][0];
        $this->assertEqualsWithDelta($total, (float) $payment['unallocated'], 0.001);
        $this->assertSame([], $payment['allocations']);
        $this->assertEqualsWithDelta($total, (float) $this->readInvoice($invoice['id'])['outstanding'], 0.001);

        // A posted payment cannot be changed beyond its allocations.
        $refused = $this->gql(self::PAYMENT_UPDATE, ['in' => [['id' => $payment['id'], 'amount' => 1]]], $this->token);
        $this->assertSame('BAD_INPUT', $this->code($refused), $refused['raw']);

        // Void the payment, then the invoice.
        $this->ok(self::PAYMENT_DELETE, ['ids' => [$payment['id']]]);
        $this->assertEqualsWithDelta($total, $this->balance($customer['id']), 0.001);
        $this->ok(self::INVOICE_DELETE, ['ids' => [$invoice['id']]]);
        $voided = $this->readInvoice($invoice['id']);
        $this->assertTrue($voided['voided']);
        $this->assertEqualsWithDelta(0.0, $this->balance($customer['id']), 0.001);
    }

    public function testPartialDeliveriesAreInvoicedAndGuarded(): void
    {
        $customer = $this->createCustomer();
        $order = $this->createOrder($customer, 3, 10.0);

        // A stale order version is refused.
        $stale = $this->gql(self::DELIVERY_CREATE, ['in' => [[
            'orderId' => $order['id'], 'orderVersion' => $order['version'] + 1, 'date' => $this->today(),
        ]]], $this->token);
        $this->assertSame('FA_REJECTED', $this->code($stale), $stale['raw']);

        $lineId = $this->orderLineIds((int) $order['id'])[0];
        $delivery = $this->ok(self::DELIVERY_CREATE, ['in' => [[
            'orderId' => $order['id'],
            'orderVersion' => $order['version'],
            'date' => $this->today(),
            'lines' => [['orderLineId' => $lineId, 'quantity' => 1]],
        ]]])['deliveryCreate'][0];
        $this->assertEquals(1, $delivery['lines'][0]['quantity']);

        $invoice = $this->ok(self::INVOICE_CREATE, ['in' => [[
            'deliveryIds' => [$delivery['id']], 'date' => $this->today(),
        ]]])['invoiceCreate'][0];
        $this->assertSame([(string) $delivery['id']], array_map('strval', $invoice['deliveryIds']));
        $this->assertEqualsWithDelta(10.0, (float) $invoice['total'], 0.5, 'one of three at 10 (plus any tax)');

        // A delivery that has been invoiced cannot be voided.
        $refused = $this->gql(self::DELIVERY_DELETE, ['ids' => [$delivery['id']]], $this->token);
        $this->assertSame('FA_REJECTED', $this->code($refused), $refused['raw']);

        // Void the invoice; then the delivery can be voided.
        $this->ok(self::INVOICE_DELETE, ['ids' => [$invoice['id']]]);
        $this->ok(self::DELIVERY_DELETE, ['ids' => [$delivery['id']]]);
    }

    public function testARoleWithoutBillingAreasIsForbidden(): void
    {
        $customer = $this->createCustomer();
        $order = $this->createOrder($customer, 1, 5.0);
        $orders = $this->login('apiorders')['accessToken'];   // SA_SALESORDER only (Release 2 seed)

        $response = $this->gql(self::INVOICE_CREATE, ['in' => [[
            'orderId' => $order['id'], 'orderVersion' => $order['version'], 'date' => $this->today(),
        ]]], $orders);
        $this->assertSame('FORBIDDEN', $this->code($response), $response['raw']);

        $response = $this->gql(self::INVOICE_EMAIL, ['ids' => [1]], $orders);
        $this->assertSame('FORBIDDEN', $this->code($response), $response['raw']);
    }

    /** @return array<string, mixed> */
    private function createCustomer(): array
    {
        $ref = $this->prefix . '-' . bin2hex(random_bytes(2));
        return $this->ok(self::CUSTOMER_CREATE, ['in' => [[
            'name' => "Billing customer $ref",
            'ref' => $ref,
            'address' => "1 Billing Street\nHosting Town",
            'salesTypeId' => '1',
            'paymentTermsId' => '1',
            'creditStatusId' => '1',
            'branch' => [
                'salesmanId' => '1', 'salesAreaId' => '1', 'taxGroupId' => '1',
                'locationId' => 'DEF', 'shipperId' => '1',
            ],
            'contact' => ['email' => strtolower($ref) . '@example.com'],
        ]]])['customerCreate'][0];
    }

    /** @return array<string, mixed> */
    private function createOrder(array $customer, int $quantity, float $price): array
    {
        $order = $this->ok(self::ORDER_CREATE, ['in' => [[
            'customerId' => $customer['id'],
            'branchId' => $customer['branches'][0]['id'],
            'orderDate' => $this->today(),
            'lines' => [['stockId' => self::ITEM, 'quantity' => $quantity, 'unitPrice' => $price]],
        ]]])['salesOrderCreate'][0];
        $this->orders[] = (int) $order['id'];
        return $order;
    }

    /** @return array<string, mixed> */
    private function readInvoice($id): array
    {
        $list = $this->ok(self::INVOICE_BY_ID, ['q' => ['selector' => json_encode(['id' => (int) $id])]])['invoiceList'];
        $this->assertCount(1, $list);
        return $list[0];
    }

    private function balance($customerId): float
    {
        $list = $this->ok(self::CUSTOMER_BALANCE, ['q' => ['selector' => json_encode(['id' => (int) $customerId])]])
            ['customerList'];
        $this->assertCount(1, $list);
        $balance = $list[0]['balance'];
        // Step 1 decides the shape: a scalar, or an object whose total is read here.
        return (float) (is_array($balance) ? ($balance['balance'] ?? $balance['total'] ?? 0) : $balance);
    }

    /** @return int[] */
    private function orderLineIds(int $orderNo): array
    {
        $statement = $this->pdo()->prepare(
            'SELECT id FROM ' . $this->table('sales_order_details') . ' WHERE trans_type = 30 AND order_no = ? ORDER BY id'
        );
        $statement->execute([$orderNo]);
        return array_map('intval', $statement->fetchAll(\PDO::FETCH_COLUMN));
    }

    /** @return int[] */
    private function ordersOfTestCustomers(): array
    {
        $statement = $this->pdo()->prepare(
            'SELECT o.order_no FROM ' . $this->table('sales_orders') . ' o JOIN ' . $this->table('debtors_master')
            . ' d ON d.debtor_no = o.debtor_no WHERE o.trans_type = 30 AND d.debtor_ref LIKE ?'
        );
        $statement->execute([$this->prefix . '%']);
        return array_map('intval', $statement->fetchAll(\PDO::FETCH_COLUMN));
    }

    private function code(array $response): ?string
    {
        return $response['body']['errors'][0]['extensions']['code'] ?? null;
    }

    /** @return array<string, mixed> the response's data */
    private function ok(string $query, array $variables): array
    {
        $response = $this->gql($query, $variables, $this->token);
        $this->assertSame(200, $response['status'], $response['raw']);
        $this->assertArrayNotHasKey('errors', $response['body'], $response['raw']);
        return $response['body']['data'];
    }

    private function pdo(): \PDO
    {
        if ($this->pdo === null) {
            $this->pdo = new \PDO(
                'mysql:host=' . getenv('FA_DB_HOST') . ';dbname=' . getenv('FA_DB_NAME'),
                (string) getenv('FA_DB_USER'),
                (string) getenv('FA_DB_PASSWORD')
            );
            $this->pdo->setAttribute(\PDO::ATTR_ERRMODE, \PDO::ERRMODE_EXCEPTION);
        }
        return $this->pdo;
    }

    private function table(string $name): string
    {
        return getenv('FA_DB_PREFIX') . $name;
    }

    /** Today as FrontAccounting's Today() sees it: PHP's default timezone. */
    private function today(): string
    {
        return date('Y-m-d');
    }
}
```

Use `FaBillingRows`' real API from Task 2: its constructor or `mark(…)`, and its `purge()`. If it needs the list of documents rather than "everything since mark", collect the ids this test created (invoices, deliveries, payments) and pass them. The rule is Release 2's: remove only what this test created, never an earlier run's rows.

The `apiorders` user (Release 2 seed) holds `SA_SALESORDER` and `SA_SALESTRANSVIEW` but none of the billing areas. If Task 2 widened that role, use a role without `SA_SALESINVOICE`, or add one to the seed.

- [ ] **Step 4: README**

After "### The panel's flow", add these sections. Keep the style of the existing ones: what each mutation does, the FrontAccounting behaviour it follows, and the refusals a client will meet.

````markdown
### Deliveries and invoices

`deliveryCreate` delivers a sales order, whole or in part, as FrontAccounting's
delivery page does. It takes `orderId` and the `orderVersion` you read (a stale
version is refused), the delivery `date`, and optionally `lines` (`orderLineId`,
`quantity`; default: everything remaining) and `closeOrder` (cancel whatever is
not delivered). Stock is checked unless the company allows negative stock.
`deliveryDelete` voids a delivery; one that has been invoiced cannot be voided.

`invoiceCreate` invoices either `deliveryIds` (one or more deliveries of the same
customer, branch and currency) or an order in one step (`orderId` +
`orderVersion`: FrontAccounting delivers what remains, then invoices it). The due
date follows the payment terms unless given. `invoiceDelete` voids an invoice; an
invoice with payments allocated to it must be deallocated first.

Every document date must fall in an open fiscal year FrontAccounting accepts, and
needs an exchange rate for the customer's currency on that date.

### Payments and allocations

`customerPaymentCreate` records a payment into a bank account (`bankAccountList`)
and allocates it to invoices you name — never automatically:

```graphql
mutation ($in: [CustomerPaymentCreateInput!]!) {
  customerPaymentCreate(input: $in) { id unallocated allocations { toId amount } }
}
```

with `{"in": [{"customerId": "12", "branchId": "12", "bankAccountId": "1",
"date": "2026-09-26", "amount": 110, "allocations": [{"invoiceId": "34", "amount": 110}]}]}`.

`customerPaymentUpdate` changes only a payment's allocations: the list replaces
them, and an empty list deallocates it. Anything else about a posted payment is
changed by voiding it (`customerPaymentDelete`) and entering it again. A
foreign-currency payment that already has allocations cannot be reallocated
(FrontAccounting would post its exchange difference twice). `allocationList` and a
customer's `balance` read the result.

### Emailing invoices

`invoiceEmail(id: [...])` sends each invoice through FrontAccounting's own invoice
report — the PDF the web UI prints, with any report override your company has —
to the customer's invoice contact (else its general contact), with the company's
BCC. Each result says whether FrontAccounting sent it and, if not, why:

```graphql
mutation { invoiceEmail(id: ["34"]) { id sent recipient messages } }
```

The report runs in a separate PHP process on the server (`bin/fa-report`), after
any writes earlier in the same request have committed; the server's PHP must be
able to send mail (`sendmail_path`).

### The billing flow

```php
[, $body] = $gql('mutation ($in: [InvoiceCreateInput!]!) { invoiceCreate(input: $in) { id total } }',
    ['in' => [['orderId' => $order['id'], 'orderVersion' => $order['version'], 'date' => date('Y-m-d')]]],
    $pair['accessToken']);
$invoice = $body['data']['invoiceCreate'][0];

$gql('mutation ($ids: [ID!]!) { invoiceEmail(id: $ids) { sent messages } }', ['ids' => [$invoice['id']]], $pair['accessToken']);

$gql('mutation ($in: [CustomerPaymentCreateInput!]!) { customerPaymentCreate(input: $in) { id } }',
    ['in' => [['customerId' => $customer['id'], 'branchId' => $customer['branches'][0]['id'],
        'bankAccountId' => 1, 'date' => date('Y-m-d'), 'amount' => $invoice['total'],
        'allocations' => [['invoiceId' => $invoice['id'], 'amount' => $invoice['total']]]]]],
    $pair['accessToken']);
```
````

Align every field name in these sections with Step 1's introspection. Change the README's "Recurring orders" paragraph from "Generating the recurring invoices comes in a later release" to "… comes in Release 4".

- [ ] **Step 5: Gates and commit**

```bash
docker/fa-graphql test && docker/fa-graphql lint && docker/fa-graphql analyze
git add -A && git commit -m "Cover the billing flow over HTTP; document deliveries, invoices, payments and emailing

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_013U8a6JAbsHc4c1KcSLiaYK"
```

The full suite must pass, and the tables must be back at their starting row counts with no caught mail left.

### Checkpoint D: final

The whole branch against the whole spec, on all four FrontAccounting × PHP combinations. Nothing merges until this is clean.

- [ ] **Independent review, on the most capable model.**
  - Build the review packages:
    - the whole branch, `feature/release-2..HEAD` (Release 3 branches from Release 2's unmerged head);
    - the part no checkpoint has reviewed yet, `<Checkpoint C head>..HEAD` (Tasks 6–7).
  - Give the reviewer both packages, this checklist, the spec, and the ledger's rulings and deferred minors to triage.
  - The reviewer weights its attention to:
    - **The CLI child (`bin/fa-report`).** Web SAPI refusal; `bin/` denied by `.htaccess`. Argument validation; no shell (`proc_open` with an argument array). `VerifiedIdentity` cleared at shutdown. `tmp/faillog.php` restored byte for byte. Nothing it prints can reach an API response. The 60 s kill. Behaviour when FrontAccounting `exit`s early (login refused, `FrontReport` access check, `check_db_error`).
    - **`invoiceEmail`.** Guarded by `SA_SALESTRANSVIEW` and `SA_SALESINVOICE`. Refused inside a transaction. Voided and unknown invoices refused. It can only email invoices of the request's company, and it logs in as the requesting user, never another.
    - **Cross-task consistency.** Every document write holds `DocumentLock`. Every billing service calls `BillingChecks` (fiscal year, exchange rates). Every write is GL-balanced. Explicit allocations only. The void guards. The areas on every resolver, including computed fields (`lines`, `allocations`, `balance`, `deliveryIds`).
    - **Every body is JSON, and company isolation holds** (one company per request).
  - Confirmed findings are fixed in one fix wave. One scoped re-review then verdicts each finding ADDRESSED or NOT ADDRESSED. Residual findings are ruled on and recorded.
- [ ] **Walk the whole Release 3 spec** section by section. For each numbered requirement, name the test that proves it, or the evidence if it can't be tested (for example `rep107` overrides). Particular attention:
  - §2.1 the lock: `DocumentLockTest` and the concurrent-invoice test;
  - §2.2 fiscal year, §2.3 exchange rates;
  - §3 each ported delivery check;

  Every deviation found is fixed, or written into the spec marked *(revised)* with its reason.

That service reprices lines, ignores `qty_done` and dates from `prepare_child`'s today, and it skips the fiscal-year, stock, on-hold and exchange-rate checks this release ports. Advance `dt_next` in the same `FaTransaction`, through FrontAccounting's connection (Release 2 spec §4.5's one-side rule), and gate each order on `sgw_sales`' `due()`.
